## Canadian Forces Employment Equity Regulations {#canadian-forces-employment-equity-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2002-421
:::

[EMPLOYMENT EQUITY ACT](/eng/acts/E-5.401)

Registration 2002-11-21

Canadian Forces Employment Equity Regulations

P.C. 2002-1957 2002-11-21
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Whereas, pursuant to paragraph 4(1)(d) of the [Employment Equity
Act](/eng/acts/E-5.401)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_SOR-2002-421_e_hq_3815){#fn_SOR-2002-421_e_hq_3815-ID0EBBCA},
the Canadian Forces have been specified as a portion of the public
sector employing one hundred or more employees by Order in Council P.C.
2002-1956 of November 21, 2002;

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_SOR-2002-421_e_hq_3815-ID0EBBCA){#fn_SOR-2002-421_e_hq_3815}S.C.
  1995, c. 44
  :::

And whereas, pursuant to subsections 41(5) and (6) of that Act, the
Governor in Council, on the recommendation of the Treasury Board and the
Minister of Labour, after consultation with the Minister of National
Defence, considers it necessary to make regulations to adapt the
provisions of that Act to accommodate the Canadian Forces, taking into
account their operational effectiveness;

Therefore, Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the
recommendation of the Treasury Board and the Minister of Labour,
pursuant to section 41 of the [Employment Equity
Act](/eng/acts/E-5.401)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_SOR-2002-421_e_hq_3815){#fn_SOR-2002-421_e_hq_3815-ID0EDAAA},
hereby makes the annexed [Canadian Forces Employment Equity
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2002-421).
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-698096 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in these Regulations.

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Employment Equity
      Act](/eng/acts/E-5.401). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canadian Forces member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canadian Forces member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual
      described in subsection 4(1). ([membre des Forces
      canadiennes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[military occupation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[military occupation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a military
      occupation set out in column 2 of Part 1 or 2 of Schedule
      1. ([groupe professionnel militaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[military occupational group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[military occupational group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      military occupational group set out in column 1 of Part 1 or 2 of
      Schedule 1. ([catégorie professionnelle
      militaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[reporting period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[reporting period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the first report that the Chief of the
        Defence Staff provides to the President of the Treasury Board
        under subsections 21(3) and (4) of the Act, the period beginning
        on the day of the coming into force of these Regulations and
        ending on March 31, 2003; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for any subsequent report, the fiscal year
        preceding that report. ([période de rapport]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other definitions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise provided, all words and expressions
  used in these Regulations have the same meaning as in the [National
  Defence Act](/eng/acts/N-5) or the regulations made under that Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions for the purposes of the Act

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of the Act
insofar as it applies to the Canadian Forces,

[[employee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[employee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Canadian Forces
    member. ([salarié]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[hired]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hired]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means enrolled as a Canadian Forces
    member under section 20 of the [National Defence
    Act](/eng/acts/N-5). ([recrutement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"},
    [engagement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
    [embauche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[occupational group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[occupational group]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a military
    occupational group. ([catégorie professionnelle]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[promoted]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[promoted]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a Canadian Forces
    member, means promoted under section 28 of the [National Defence
    Act](/eng/acts/N-5). ([avancement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
    [promotion]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[salary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[salary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the pay issued to Canadian
    Forces members at the rates and under the conditions prescribed
    under subsection 12(3) and section 35 of the [National Defence
    Act](/eng/acts/N-5). ([rémunération]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[terminated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[terminated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    [[release]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1) of the
    [National Defence Act](/eng/acts/N-5). ([cessation de
    fonctions]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [cessation
    d'emploi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

## [Purpose]{.HTitleText1} {#h-698117 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation of the Act

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Pursuant
  to section 41 of the Act, the purpose of these Regulations is to adapt
  the provisions of the Act to accommodate the Canadian Forces, taking
  into account the need for their operational effectiveness.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise indicated, no other regulations made
  under the Act apply to the Canadian Forces.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Members of the Forces

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Act
  and these Regulations apply to Canadian Forces members --- both
  officers and non-commissioned members --- who are serving

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the regular force; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the reserve force, other than those declared
    non-effective strength.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special force

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Act and these Regulations do not apply to
  Canadian Forces members who are serving in the special force.

## [General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-698130 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligations of Chief of the Defence Staff

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** The Chief of the Defence
Staff, acting within the scope of the Chief of the Defence Staff's
powers, duties and functions under section 18 of the [National Defence
Act](/eng/acts/N-5), is responsible, in relation to the Canadian Forces,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} for carrying out the obligations of an employer under
  the Act; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the application of these Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Scope of obligation

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of section
6 of the Act, the obligation to implement employment equity does not
require the Chief of the Defence Staff

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to enrol, re-engage or promote persons without basing
  the enrolment, re-engagement or promotion on the requirements
  specified in section 7; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to undertake promotion campaigns or other measures to
  increase the degree of representation in the Canadian Forces of
  persons who do not meet the requirements specified in section 7.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualification or eligibility criteria

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** The enrolment,
re-engagement or promotion of any person in the Canadian Forces shall be
based on the following requirements, to the extent that they are
consistent with the [Canadian Human Rights Act](/eng/acts/H-6):

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicable criteria in the regulations, orders
  and instructions made under sections 12 and 18 of the [National
  Defence Act](/eng/acts/N-5); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the liability to perform any lawful duty in
  accordance with section 33 of that Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation of section 8 of the Act

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of section
8 of the Act, the release of Canadian Forces members under an initiative
to reduce the number of members of the Canadian Forces, or the number of
members of any unit, element or military occupational group of the
Canadian Forces, is not an employment barrier within the meaning of the
Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of the Commission and Tribunal

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of section
33 of the Act, the Canadian Human Rights Commission, its officers and
any other person acting on behalf of or under the direction of the
Commission, and the members of an Employment Equity Review Tribunal
established under subsection 28(1) of the Act and any other person
acting on behalf of or under the direction of such a Tribunal, may not,
in exercising the powers conferred on the Commission or the Tribunal
under sections 25 or 26 and 30 of the Act, respectively, give a
direction or make an order that would

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} require the Chief of the Defence Staff to enrol,
  re-engage or promote persons without basing the enrolment,
  re-engagement or promotion on the requirements specified in section 7;
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} otherwise prejudice the operational effectiveness of
  the Canadian Forces.

## [Collection of Workforce Information]{.HTitleText1} {#h-698149 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Questionnaire

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before
  preparing the employment equity plan required by section 10 of the
  Act, the Chief of the Defence Staff shall conduct a workforce survey
  by providing to each Canadian Forces member a workforce survey
  questionnaire that asks if the member is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an Aboriginal person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a member of a visible minority; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a person with a disability.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Member identification

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The questionnaire shall include questions that
  provide a means of identifying the Canadian Forces member who returns
  it, by name or otherwise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional questions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The questionnaire may include additional questions
  relating to employment equity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The questionnaire shall include the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the definitions [[Aboriginal
    peoples]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, [[members of visible
    minorities]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} and [[persons with
    disabilities]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as set out in section 3 of the
    Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the definition [[designated
    groups]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as set out in section 3 of the Act
    and the statement that a person may be a member of more than one
    designated group;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement that responses to the questions
    referred to in subsection (1.1) are mandatory;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a statement that responses to the questions
    referred to in subsections (1) and (2) are voluntary; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a statement containing the information referred to
    in section 14.

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equity plan

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** When the Chief of the
Defence Staff reviews the employment equity plan, the Chief of the
Defence Staff is not required to conduct a new workforce survey if the
previous survey results have been kept up to date in accordance with
section 13.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Updating

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** The Chief of the Defence
Staff shall keep the workforce survey results up to date

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by providing a workforce survey questionnaire

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to all new Canadian Forces members on their
    enrolment,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to any Canadian Forces member who wishes to change
    any information that they previously submitted on a questionnaire,
    or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to any Canadian Forces member who requests one;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} by making the necessary adjustments to the survey
  results to take into account the responses to a questionnaire referred
  to in paragraph (a); and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} by making the necessary adjustments to the survey
  results to take into account members of designated groups who have
  been released from the Canadian Forces.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Personal information

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** Information collected
under sections 10 and 13 is personal information within the meaning of
section 3 of the [Privacy Act](/eng/acts/P-21) and shall not be used or
disclosed except in accordance with that Act.

## [Workforce Analysis]{.HTitleText1} {#h-698179 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Based
  on the information collected under sections 10 and 13 and on relevant
  information contained in any other employment records of the Canadian
  Forces, the Chief of the Defence Staff shall conduct an analysis of
  the Canadian Forces workforce to determine

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each military occupational group, the number of
    positions held by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} women,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} Aboriginal persons,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} persons who are members of visible minorities,
      and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} persons with disabilities; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the degree of underrepresentation of the persons
    referred to in paragraph (a) by comparing the representation of each
    designated group in each military occupational group to its
    representation within whichever of the following groups is the most
    appropriate as a basis of comparison, namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the Canadian workforce, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} those segments of the Canadian workforce that
      are identifiable on the basis of qualification or eligibility
      criteria, including the requirements specified in section 7, and
      from which the Chief of the Defence Staff may reasonably be
      expected to draw Canadian Forces members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Underrepresentation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In making a determination under paragraph (1)(b), the
  Chief of the Defence Staff shall use

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the labour market information made available by the
    Minister of Labour under subsection 42(3) of the Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other statistically-reliable information that
    is available to the public and that the Minister of Labour
    determines relevant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equity plan

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} When the Chief of the Defence Staff reviews the
  employment equity plan, the Chief of the Defence Staff is not required
  to conduct a new workforce analysis if the results of the previous
  analysis are up to date as a result of periodic revisions that take
  into account the updating of the workforce survey results in
  accordance with section 13.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-201, s. 10(E)](#1315201-1316539){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1315201-1316539.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-15-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 15"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers and compliance audits

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  exercising any powers with respect to the application of the Act or
  these Regulations to the Canadian Forces, including the powers under
  sections 22 and 23 of the Act, the Canadian Human Rights Commission,
  its officers and any other person acting on behalf of or under the
  direction of the Commission shall take into account

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the fact that Canadian Forces members who may be
    members of one or more of the designated groups of Aboriginal
    peoples, persons with disabilities and members of visible
    minorities, may choose not to identify themselves as such or not to
    agree to be identified as such;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the enrolment, re-engagement and promotion
    requirements specified in section 7; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the need for the operational effectiveness of the
    Canadian Forces.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Member identification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, in exercising any power or
  performing any duty or function under the Act or these Regulations,
  the Chief of the Defence Staff is subject to the obligation under
  subsection 25(1.3) of the Act not to identify individual members of
  the Canadian Forces who are members of designated groups and who have
  not identified themselves as such or agreed to be identified by the
  Canadian Forces as such.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-201, s. 10(E)](#1315201-1316542){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1315201-1316542.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 16"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Analysis summary

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** The Chief of the Defence
Staff shall prepare a summary of the results of the workforce analysis
for use in the preparation of the employment equity plan required by
section 10 of the Act.

## [Review of Employment Systems, Policies and Practices]{.HTitleText1} {#h-698207 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  workforce analysis conducted under section 15 indicates that there is
  underrepresentation of persons in designated groups in any military
  occupational group, the Chief of the Defence Staff shall review the
  Canadian Forces employment systems, policies and practices to
  determine whether any of them is an employment barrier against persons
  in designated groups.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Relevant matters

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} To determine whether the employment systems, policies
  and practices that apply to those military occupational groups in
  which there is underrepresentation of persons in designated groups are
  employment barriers, the Chief of the Defence Staff shall review them
  with respect to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} recruitment, selection and enrolment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} training and development;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} career management and promotions;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} re-engagement and release; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the reasonable accommodation of the special needs
    of persons in designated groups.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New systems

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, following a review under subsection (2), the
  Chief of the Defence Staff implements new employment systems, policies
  and practices relating to the matters referred to in that subsection,
  the Chief of the Defence Staff shall also review the new employment
  systems, policies and practices in order to determine whether they are
  employment barriers against persons in designated groups.

## [Employment Equity Records]{.HTitleText1} {#h-698220 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of records

**[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** In accordance with
section 17 of the Act, the Chief of the Defence Staff shall establish
and maintain records that contain the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} information indicating, for each Canadian Forces
  member, whether the member has identified themself as a member of one
  or more of the designated groups;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the military occupation of each Canadian Forces
  member;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the military rank of each Canadian Forces member, as
  set out in the schedule to the [National Defence Act](/eng/acts/N-5),
  that determines that member's salary;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the promotion history of each Canadian Forces member;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the workforce survey questionnaire that was
  provided to each Canadian Forces member and of any other information
  used in the workforce analysis;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the summary of the results of the workforce analysis
  required by section 17;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the measures undertaken in conducting the employment
  systems review;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} the employment equity plan of the Canadian Forces and
  the information used to prepare it;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} the measures undertaken to monitor the implementation
  of the employment equity plan in accordance with paragraph 12(b) of
  the Act; and

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} the information provided to the Canadian Forces
  members in accordance with section 14 of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-201, s. 4](#1315189-1316307){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1315189-1316307.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention of records after release

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 6(3) of the
  [Privacy Act](/eng/acts/P-21) and section 4 of the [Privacy
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-83-508), the records containing the
  information referred to in paragraphs 19(a) to (d) shall be retained
  throughout the period during which the person to whom the records
  relate is employed as a Canadian Forces member and for two years after
  the date of their release.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of retention

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 6(3) of the [Privacy
  Act](/eng/acts/P-21) and section 4 of the [Privacy
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-83-508), the records containing the
  information referred to in paragraphs 19(e) to (j) shall be retained
  for two years after the period covered by the employment equity plan
  to which the records relate.

## [Consultation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-698238 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation process

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Chief of the Defence Staff shall consult Canadian Forces members, and
  may consult representatives of designated groups, to obtain their
  views on and assistance in any matter concerning

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the implementation of employment equity in the
    Canadian Forces workforce and the communication of matters relating
    to employment equity to Canadian Forces members; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the preparation, implementation and revision of the
    employment equity plan of the Canadian Forces.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation mechanism

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Chief of the Defence Staff shall establish a
  mechanism for consulting Canadian Forces members.

## [Compliance]{.HTitleText1} {#h-698246 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security requirement

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Canadian Human Rights Commission, any of its officers and any other
  person acting on behalf of or under the direction of the Commission,
  as well as any member of an Employment Equity Review Tribunal
  established under subsection 28(1) of the Act and any other person
  acting on behalf of or under the direction of such a Tribunal, who
  receives or obtains information for the purposes of the Act shall
  satisfy, with respect to access to and use of that information, any
  requirements in the regulations, orders and instructions made under
  the [National Defence Act](/eng/acts/N-5) that relate to the security
  of the places to be entered or the records, books of account or other
  documents to be examined.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate means

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the requirements referred to in subsection (1)
  restrict or limit access to information that is necessary for the
  application of the Act or these Regulations, the Chief of the Defence
  Staff shall provide other means, consistent with the regulations,
  orders and instructions made under the [National Defence
  Act](/eng/acts/N-5), that will permit the persons described in
  subsection (1) to carry out their responsibilities under the Act.

## [Reports]{.HTitleText1} {#h-698254 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** The contents of the
report required by subparagraphs 21(2)(a)(i), (iii) and (v) of the Act
shall be provided using the forms in Schedules 3 to 15 for each
reporting period.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Summary of representation

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** The Chief of the Defence
Staff shall provide the information required by

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} Part 1 of Schedule 5 with respect to the number and
  degree of representation of officers referred to in subsection 4(1)
  for the reporting period; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} Part 2 of Schedule 5 with respect to the number and
  degree of representation of non-commissioned members referred to in
  subsection 4(1) for the reporting period.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representation by category

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** The Chief of the Defence
Staff shall provide the information required by

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} Schedule 3 with respect to the number and degree of
  representation of Canadian Forces members referred to in paragraph
  4(1)(a) for the reporting period; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} Schedule 4 with respect to the number and degree of
  representation of Canadian Forces members referred to in paragraph
  4(1)(b) for the reporting period.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Military occupational group

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** In the forms set out in
Schedules 3 to 5 and 8 to 15, the Chief of the Defence Staff shall
indicate the military occupational group of Canadian Forces members by
referring to the military occupation that corresponds to the job
performed by the members.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representation by rank

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** The Chief of the Defence
Staff shall provide the following information for the reporting period
for each military rank set out in the schedule to the [National Defence
Act](/eng/acts/N-5):

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the form set out in Schedule 6, the number and
  degree of representation of Canadian Forces members referred to in
  paragraph 4(1)(a); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the form set out in Schedule 7, the number and
  degree of representation of Canadian Forces members referred to in
  paragraph 4(1)(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-201, s. 6](#1315193-1316313){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1315193-1316313.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-27-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 27"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enrolment, promotion, re-engagement and
release

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Chief of the Defence Staff shall provide the information required for
  the reporting period with respect to the number and degree of
  representation of Canadian Forces members referred to in paragraph
  4(1)(a)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the form set out in Schedule 8, for enrolments;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the form set out in Schedule 10, for promotions;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the form set out in Schedule 12, for
    re-engagements; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the form set out in Schedule 14, for releases.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Chief of the Defence Staff shall provide the
  information required for the reporting period with respect to the
  number and degree of representation of Canadian Forces members
  referred to in paragraph 4(1)(b)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the form set out in Schedule 9, for enrolments;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the form set out in Schedule 11, for promotions;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the form set out in Schedule 13, for
    re-engagements; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the form set out in Schedule 15, for releases.

## [Review]{.HTitleText1} {#h-698283 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** After any amendment to
the [Employment Equity Act](/eng/acts/E-5.401) or at the end of a period
not exceeding five years after the coming into force of these
Regulations, and at the end of every five-year period after that, the
Chief of the Defence Staff shall conduct a review of the provisions and
operation of these Regulations, including the effect of the Regulations.

## [Coming into Force]{.HTitleText1} {#h-698286 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coming into force

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations come
into force on the day on which they are registered.

:::::: section
::::: {#698289 .Schedule}
::: {}
